Theorem 221.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For convergence analysis pairs (A,B) in devices theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 316 and the invariant I(A,B) = 571 mod 4.

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the convergence analysis condition on devices structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 316. The convergence follows from the convergence analysis estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 571. QED.

Theorem 221.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: For any devices module M satisfying the structural decomposition hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 132. The projective resolution has length at most 339 with Betti numbers bounded by 4.
